# CR733 Run 1 — P0 blockers

## Status

**HARD STOP — P1 must not begin.** The regenerated, pinned census found 229 reachable `GameState` fields with no single existing final ordinary mutation authority. Those fields have 1,973 reachable write-family hits. Assigning an authority or a resolved-command family now would invent ownership that the source does not provide.

This report deliberately records the incomplete matrix rather than force-fitting a command row. The machine-readable, exact companion list is [`blocked_write_sites.json`](../../crates/engine/tests/fixtures/cr733/blocked_write_sites.json); the table below repeats every hit for review without requiring a rerun.

## Census evidence

- Base commit: `e48c7d6e1861d735f1b39aa5fd60d5d79e53b418`.
- Stable census hash: `c6afb7c698f464d2a84c7733ea7f71197877a66d4a29a44b33960f399a3ac376`.
- Census population: 3,362 sites; 8,662 reachable functions; 256 `GameState` fields.
- Family counts: write=2,368, allocator=168, rng=74, event_emission=705, information=47.

The detector is deliberately an over-approximation: it is name-based, has no trait/macro/field-alias resolution, and may over-report a receiver. That is a reason to review a record, not to silently discard it. It does not prove the absence of external effects; a source scan of the engine game modules found only test/tool filesystem reads, not a production external-state mutation in this closure.

The decisive examples are not isolated misses: `waiting_for`, `objects`, `players`, `stack`, and multiple trigger/continuation containers are written directly by many functions. They cannot truthfully be assigned one final authority until a reviewed factoring design exists.

## Required next decision

A reviewed CR733 prerequisite revision must first select and introduce final semantic mutation seams for every blocked field family, especially object/zone state, player resources, stack and priority, trigger/LKI settlement, and continuation/prompt state. Only then can P0 replace the `blocked_missing_final_authority` records with one authority plus one command family and composition policy. Do not begin identity, ordinal, mana-provenance, or command-applier work against this incomplete surface.
